Every software developer is taught thesame basic rule. Code only does exactlywhat you tell it to do. If there's abug, you fix it. If an API changes, youupdate the connection. Traditionalsoftware is simply a sequence ofpredefined instructions. But that ruleis changing. We are moving beyond staticprograms and entering the era of agenticAI means system where you don't writeevery step. You simply define theobjective. Imagine building a systemwhere you say analyze our sales data,find lowest performing region and launcha targeted email campaign to improvesales. A traditional program or chatbotcan't do that. It doesn't understand thebigger objective. But an AI agent can.It decides which tool to use, queriesthe database, analyzes the trends,drafts the email, connects to themailing API and evaluates the result. Ifsomething fails, it analyzes the error,adjusts the approach and tries again. Ittransforms software from a passive toolinto autonomous coworker. Right now most peoplethink AI is just tools like chat GPTanswering questions. You type a promptand the AI gives a response and that'sit. But the next wave of AI is verydifferent. Imagine an AI that doesn'tjust answer your questions. It planstask, makes decision and completes workon its own. Instead of being just atool, it starts behaving more like adigital worker. And that's where thedifference between generative AI andagentic AI begins. Understanding thisshift is important because it couldshape the future of technology, jobs,and productivity. So, in this video,we'll break down what generative AI is,what agentic AI means, the keydifferences between them, and why manyexperts believe agentic AI is the nextmajor evolution of artificialintelligence. So, let's start with theAI most people already know, generativeAI. Generative AI is a type ofartificial intelligence that can createnew content based on patterns it haslearned from massive data sets. Insteadof just analyzing information, it canactually generate new outputs. Theseoutputs can include texts, images, code,audio, and video. Some of the mostpopular generative AI tools todayinclude Chad GPT, Midjourney, and Dali.These tools can produce incrediblymassive results in seconds. For example,you can ask AI. These tools can produceincredibly impressive results inseconds. For example, you can ask AI towrite an email, generate marketingcontent, create an image, summarize adocument, or even explain a complextopic. And almost instantly, the AIproduces an answer. But here is oneimportant limitation. Generative AI isprompt based. It only works when youtell it what to do. It doesn't takeinitiative. It doesn't plan task. and itdoesn't execute actions in the realworld. It simply generates an output inresponse to a prompt. And that's wherethe next evolution of AI begins. Ifgenerative AI is about creating content,the next generation of AI is aboutsomething much bigger. It's aboutgetting work done. And this brings us toagentic AI. Agentic AI refers to AIsystems that can act as autonomousagents. Instead of simply responding toprompts, these systems can set goals,break complex task into smaller steps,make decisions, use external tools, andexecute actions. In simple terms,generative AI answers questions. AgenticAI solves problems. Let's look at asimple example. Imagine you tell an AI,"Plan a 3-day trip to Japan under$1,500."A generative AI system might respondwith suggestions like places to visit,travel tips, hotel recommendations. Butan agent AI system would go muchfurther. It could search for flights,compare hotel prices, build a completeitinerary, book reservations, and sendyou the final travel plan allautomatically. Instead of justsuggesting ideas, the AI actuallycompletes the task. That's the keydifference. So, what really separatesgenerative AI from agentic AI? Let'sbreak it down in simple terms. First,interaction. Generative AI is promptbased. You ask a question and the AIgives a response. Agentic AI isgoal-based. You give it an objective andthe system figures out how to achieveit. Second, autonomy. Generative AI haslow autonomy. It depends completely onour user instructions. Agentic AI hashigher autonomy. It can plan multiplesteps and take actions independently.Number three, execution. Generative AImainly produces content. Agentic AIperforms actions and executes workflows.Because of this, many experts believeagentic AI represents the next majorphase of artificial intelligence. Now,let's see why agentic AI is a big deal.The reason agentic AI is getting so muchattention is because it moves AI frombeing just a tool. It becomes a digitalworker. Instead of helping with onesmall task at a time, AI could startmanaging entire workflows. For example,in marketing, an AI agent research atopic, write a blog post, generateimages, schedule social media post, andanalyze engagement data allautomatically. In software development,an AI agent could analyze productrequirements, generate code, test thecode, detect bugs, and deploy updates.This level of automation coulddramatically increase productivityacross industry. So are we alreadyseeing agentic AI? The idea of agenticAI isn't just theoretical. We're alreadystarting to see early experiments.Projects like AutoGPT, Baby AGI areexamples of systems designed to operatewith minimal human input. These toolsuse powerful language models like thosebehind chat GPD, but add extra layers toallow them to plan task, prioritizeactions, and execute workflows. Rightnow, these systems are stillexperimental, but they give us a glimpseof where AI development is heading. So,what is the future of AI? The future ofartificial intelligence will likelycombine both approaches. Generative AIwill continue to power creativity,content generation, explanation andideas while agentic AI will powerautomation, task execution, workflowmanagement. In other words, generativeAI creates the ideas and agentic AIcarries them out. So the differencebetween these two types of AI can besummarized simply into generative AIcreates content and agentic AI takesaction. And as AI continues to evolve,we may soon see systems that don't justanswer questions, but actually workalongside humans to complete complextask. Understanding the shift isimportant because it could shape thenext generation of AI tools, businesses,and careers. So if you found thisexplanation helpful, let me know in thecomments.
